- **chat**: 39 documented top-level params exercised; schema-derived values for: prompt, session_id, usage, include_reasoning, prompt_cache_options, meta, echo_upstream_request.

- **messages**: 20 documented top-level params exercised; schema-derived values for: prompt, max_tokens, session_id, user, prompt_cache_key, usage, meta, echo_upstream_request.

## Per provider
